I also don't really struggle with waiting my turn in lines or conversations.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself worrying excessively about everyday things, and do you feel like this worry is difficult to control?\n\nPatient: No, I don't really experience excessive or uncontrollable worry about everyday things. I haven't had any periods of unusually high mood, irritability, or extreme energy surges that lasted for several days.\n\nDoctor: Does this constant fidgeting cause difficulties for you in more than one setting, such as at work, at home, or in social situations?\n\nPatient: To be honest, I'm not really sure about that. I haven't really thought about whether my fidgeting causes problems in different places like work or at home.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel internally restless or find yourself leaving your seat in situations where you are expected to remain seated, such as during meetings, lectures, or movies?\n\nPatient: Yes, absolutely.
